//@ pragma IconTheme Papirus-Dark

import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Wayland
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Controls

ShellRoot {
    id: shell
    property bool forcedOpen: false
    property int desktopEntriesRevision: 0

    Connections {
        target: DesktopEntries
        function onApplicationsChanged(): void { shell.desktopEntriesRevision++; }
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "dock"
        function toggle(): void { shell.forcedOpen = !shell.forcedOpen; }
    }

    Variants {
        model: Quickshell.screens

        PanelWindow {
            id: dock
            required property var modelData
            property bool hoverExpanded: false
            readonly property bool expanded: shell.forcedOpen || hoverExpanded
            property var hoveredEntry: null

            function clearHoveredEntry(entry): void {
                if (hoveredEntry === entry) hoveredEntry = null;
            }

            screen: modelData
            visible: true
            anchors.left: true
            anchors.top: true
            anchors.bottom: true
            implicitWidth: expanded ? 76 : 3
            mask: Region {
                width: 3
                height: dock.height
                Region {
                    y: panel.y
                    width: dock.expanded ? dock.width : 0
                    height: panel.height
                }
            }
            color: "transparent"
            exclusionMode: ExclusionMode.Ignore
            exclusiveZone: 0
            WlrLayershell.namespace: "dock"
            WlrLayershell.layer: WlrLayer.Overlay
            WlrLayershell.keyboardFocus: WlrKeyboardFocus.None

            Timer {
                id: tooltipDelay
                readonly property var entry: dock.expanded ? dock.hoveredEntry : null
                interval: 100
                onEntryChanged: {
                    tooltip.visible = false;
                    if (entry) restart();
                    else stop();
                }
                onTriggered: tooltip.visible = entry !== null
            }

            PopupWindow {
                id: tooltip
                visible: false
                color: "transparent"
                mask: Region {}
                implicitWidth: tooltipLabel.implicitWidth + 20
                implicitHeight: tooltipLabel.implicitHeight + 12

                anchor {
                    window: dock
                    edges: Edges.Top | Edges.Right
                    gravity: Edges.Bottom | Edges.Right
                    adjustment: PopupAdjustment.SlideY
                    rect.width: 1
                    rect.height: 1
                    onAnchoring: {
                        if (!tooltipDelay.entry) return;
                        const icon = tooltipDelay.entry.tooltipIcon;
                        const center = dock.contentItem.mapFromItem(icon, 0, icon.height / 2);
                        tooltip.anchor.rect.x = dock.width - 5;
                        tooltip.anchor.rect.y = Math.round(center.y - tooltip.height / 2);
                    }
                }

                Rectangle {
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    radius: 6
                    color: "#1b1b1b"
                    border.color: "#26ffffff"

                    Text {
                        id: tooltipLabel
                        anchors.centerIn: parent
                        text: tooltipDelay.entry ? tooltipDelay.entry.appName : ""
                        color: "#eeeeee"
                        font.family: "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font"
                        font.pixelSize: 12
                    }
                }

                Connections {
                    target: windows
                    enabled: tooltip.visible
                    function onContentYChanged(): void { tooltip.anchor.updateAnchor(); }
                }
            }

            // The full-height edge and visible dock receive input; everything else passes through.
            Item {
                anchors.fill: parent

                HoverHandler {
                    id: hover
                    onHoveredChanged: {
                        if (hovered) {
                            hideDelay.stop();
                            revealDelay.start();
                        } else {
                            revealDelay.stop();
                            hideDelay.start();
                        }
                    }
                }

                Timer {
                    id: revealDelay
                    interval: 100
                    onTriggered: dock.hoverExpanded = true
                }

                Timer {
                    id: hideDelay
                    interval: 350
                    onTriggered: dock.hoverExpanded = false
                }

                Rectangle {
                    id: panel
                    x: dock.expanded ? 8 : -60
                    y: (parent.height - height) / 2
                    width: 60
                    height: Math.min(Math.max(64, windows.contentHeight + 16), Math.max(64, dock.height - 120))
                    radius: 12
                    color: "#1b1b1b"
                    border.color: "#26ffffff"
                    opacity: dock.expanded ? 1 : 0

                    Behavior on x { NumberAnimation { duration: 150; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }
                    Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: 120 } }

                    ListView {
                        id: windows
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        anchors.margins: 8
                        spacing: 4
                        clip: true
                        boundsBehavior: Flickable.StopAtBounds
                        model: ToplevelManager.toplevels

                        delegate: Button {
                            id: entry
                            required property var modelData
                            readonly property var desktopEntry: {
                                // Lookup methods do not expose binding dependencies themselves.
                                shell.desktopEntriesRevision;
                                return DesktopEntries.heuristicLookup(modelData.appId);
                            }
                            readonly property string appName: desktopEntry ? desktopEntry.name : (modelData.appId || "Application")
                            readonly property string iconSource: desktopEntry && desktopEntry.icon ? Quickshell.iconPath(desktopEntry.icon, true) : ""

                            width: ListView.view.width
                            height: 48
                            padding: 0
                            hoverEnabled: true
                            focusPolicy: Qt.NoFocus
                            Accessible.name: appName + ": " + modelData.title
                            onClicked: modelData.activate()

                            readonly property var tooltipIcon: appIcon
                            onHoveredChanged: {
                                if (hovered) dock.hoveredEntry = entry;
                                else dock.clearHoveredEntry(entry);
                            }
                            Component.onDestruction: dock.clearHoveredEntry(entry)

                            background: Rectangle {
                                radius: 8
                                color: entry.down ? "#527ebae4"
                                    : entry.hovered ? "#26ffffff"
                                    : entry.modelData.activated ? "#407ebae4" : "transparent"
                                border.color: entry.modelData.activated ? "#7ebae4" : "transparent"
                                Behavior on color { ColorAnimation { duration: 120 } }
                            }

                            contentItem: Item {
                                Image {
                                    id: appIcon
                                    anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                                    y: 5
                                    width: 30
                                    height: 30
                                    source: entry.iconSource
                                    sourceSize.width: 30
                                    sourceSize.height: 30
                                    fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectFit
                                    visible: status === Image.Ready
                                }

                                Text {
                                    anchors.fill: appIcon
                                    visible: !appIcon.visible
                                    text: entry.appName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()
                                    color: "#eeeeee"
                                    font.family: "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font"
                                    font.pixelSize: 20
                                    horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                                    verticalAlignment: Text.AlignVCenter
                                }

                                Rectangle {
                                    anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                                    anchors.bottom: parent.bottom
                                    anchors.bottomMargin: 3
                                    width: entry.modelData.activated ? 14 : 4
                                    height: 3
                                    radius: 2
                                    color: entry.modelData.activated ? "#7ebae4" : "#bfbfbf"
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        ScrollIndicator.vertical: ScrollIndicator {}
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

test_dock.py

quickshell/dock/tests/test_dock.py · 110 lines

View on GitHub 
"""QML wiring and actual JavaScript handler checks; no compositor required."""

import re
import subprocess
import unittest
from pathlib import Path


SOURCE = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "shell.qml").read_text()


def block(marker):
    start = SOURCE.index("{", SOURCE.index(marker)) + 1
    depth = 1
    for end in range(start, len(SOURCE)):
        depth += (SOURCE[end] == "{") - (SOURCE[end] == "}")
        if not depth:
            return SOURCE[start:end]
    raise AssertionError(f"Unclosed block: {marker}")


class DockTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def javascript(self, code):
        subprocess.run(
            ["node", "-e", "const assert = require('node:assert/strict');\n" + code],
            check=True,
        )

    def test_startup_edge_and_ipc(self):
        self.assertIn("visible: true", SOURCE)
        for edge in ("left", "top", "bottom"):
            self.assertIn(f"anchors.{edge}: true", SOURCE)
        self.assertIn("implicitWidth: expanded ? 76 : 3", SOURCE)
        self.assertIn("shell.forcedOpen || hoverExpanded", SOURCE)
        reveal = re.search(r"onTriggered: (dock\.hoverExpanded = true)", SOURCE).group(
            1
        )
        self.javascript(
            "const shell = { forcedOpen: false };"
            "const dock = { hoverExpanded: false };"
            f"const toggle = () => {{{block('function toggle()')}}};"
            f"const reveal = () => {{ {reveal}; }};"
            "reveal(); assert.equal(dock.hoverExpanded, true);"
            "toggle(); assert.equal(shell.forcedOpen, true);"
            "toggle(); assert.equal(shell.forcedOpen, false);"
        )

    def test_one_tooltip_per_screen(self):
        self.assertEqual(SOURCE.count("PopupWindow {"), 1)
        self.assertLess(SOURCE.index("PopupWindow {"), SOURCE.index("delegate: Button"))
        self.assertIn("mask: Region {}", block("PopupWindow {"))
        self.assertIn("interval: 100", block("            Timer {"))
        self.assertIn("dock.expanded ? dock.hoveredEntry : null", SOURCE)

    def test_hover_switch_removal_and_delay(self):
        clear = block("function clearHoveredEntry")
        change = block("onEntryChanged:")
        hover = block("readonly property var tooltipIcon")
        self.assertIn("Component.onDestruction: dock.clearHoveredEntry(entry)", SOURCE)
        self.javascript(
            "const tooltip = { visible: true }; let restarts = 0, stops = 0;"
            "const restart = () => restarts++; const stop = () => stops++;"
            f"const change = entry => {{{change}}};"
            "const dock = { hoveredEntry: null };"
            f"dock.clearHoveredEntry = entry => {{ let hoveredEntry = dock.hoveredEntry; {clear};"
            "dock.hoveredEntry = hoveredEntry; };"
            f"const hover = (entry, hovered) => {{{hover}}};"
            "const a = {}, b = {}; hover(a, true); change(a);"
            "assert.equal(tooltip.visible, false); assert.equal(restarts, 1);"
            "hover(b, true); hover(a, false); assert.equal(dock.hoveredEntry, b);"
            "change(b); assert.equal(restarts, 2);"
            "dock.clearHoveredEntry(a); assert.equal(dock.hoveredEntry, b);"
            "dock.clearHoveredEntry(b); change(null);"
            "assert.equal(dock.hoveredEntry, null); assert.equal(stops, 1);"
        )

    def test_position_and_scroll(self):
        anchor = block("onAnchoring:")
        self.assertIn(
            "function onContentYChanged(): void { tooltip.anchor.updateAnchor(); }",
            SOURCE,
        )
        self.assertIn("adjustment: PopupAdjustment.SlideY", SOURCE)
        self.javascript(
            "for (const y of [60, 250, 700]) for (const height of [24, 40]) {"
            "const icon = { height: 30 };"
            "const tooltipDelay = { entry: { tooltipIcon: icon } };"
            "const tooltip = { height, anchor: { rect: {} } };"
            "const dock = { width: 76, contentItem: { mapFromItem: () => ({ y }) } };"
            f"const anchor = () => {{{anchor}}}; anchor();"
            "assert.equal(tooltip.anchor.rect.x + 1 - 68, 4);"
            "assert.equal(tooltip.anchor.rect.y + height / 2, y);"
            "tooltipDelay.entry = null; anchor(); }"
        )

    def test_async_desktop_entry_refresh(self):
        self.assertIn(
            "shell.desktopEntriesRevision;",
            block("readonly property var desktopEntry:"),
        )
        self.assertIn("DesktopEntries.heuristicLookup(modelData.appId)", SOURCE)
        self.javascript(
            "const shell = { desktopEntriesRevision: 0 };"
            f"const refresh = () => {{{block('function onApplicationsChanged()')}}};"
            "refresh(); refresh(); assert.equal(shell.desktopEntriesRevision, 2);"
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

quickshell/lockscreen/README.md · 68 lines

View on GitHub 
# Quickshell lock screen

A Sway lock screen with per-monitor video backgrounds, clock/date, PAM authentication,
and animated password feedback. The layout adapts to small displays and long PAM messages.

## Setup

1. Deploy `quickshell/` to `~/.config/quickshell/` and `sway/scripts/lockscreen.sh` to
   `~/.config/sway/scripts/lockscreen.sh` using `placements/linux.txt`.
2. Rebuild NixOS to install the PAM service and Quickshell multimedia wrapper.
3. Test manually before relying on idle locking or suspend:

   ```sh
   ~/.config/sway/scripts/lockscreen.sh
   ```

The existing `Mod+Ctrl+Q` shortcut and swayidle configuration use this launcher.
Use the launcher rather than starting QML directly: it waits for compositor-confirmed locking.
Quickshell and its PAM service are required; missing dependencies or failed startup return an error.
There is no fallback locker.

## Behavior

- An inline Bash command uses GNU `find` and `shuf` to select a random readable MP4 recursively
  under `~/Wallpapers` during startup. Null-separated paths preserve special filenames.
  Video initializes only after locking, then fades in over a dark gradient when its first frame
  arrives. The gradient remains if media is missing or fails; media never gates lock readiness.
- The password input stays focused but hidden. Enter submits; Escape clears it and resets feedback.
  Additional PAM prompts are supported; prompts requesting visible responses show an input field.
- Typing pulses a circle, checking shows an ellipsis, failure turns it red and shakes it,
  and success shows a green checkmark before fading out.
- Configuration reload is disabled while locked; no IPC unlock method is exposed.

## Diagnostics and limitations

Logs, including video playback errors: `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/quickshell-lockscreen.log`.
Startup waits default to 10 seconds, configurable with `QUICKSHELL_LOCK_TIMEOUT`.
Duplicate requests verify the existing locker's secure state. A timeout or failed verification
returns an error, not a claim that the session is locked.

Swayidle's `before-sleep` integration is best-effort: a failed or slow lock does not guarantee
suspend will be canceled. Waybar's idle inhibitor does not disable before-sleep locking.

If the locker crashes after securing the screen, Sway keeps the session locked. Recovery may
require terminating the graphical session from another TTY, which closes its applications.

Password fields and pending references are cleared, but Qt/JavaScript does not guarantee secure
memory wiping or locked password buffers. This is not password-memory-security parity with
swaylock. Treat dumps as sensitive and verify swap/hibernation encryption separately.

## Testing

From the repository root, with Python 3, Node.js, Bash, ShellCheck, util-linux,
and GNU findutils/coreutils:

```sh
python3 -B -m unittest discover -s quickshell/lockscreen/tests -v
bash -n sway/scripts/lockscreen.sh
shellcheck sway/scripts/lockscreen.sh
```

Tests use mocks; they do not lock or suspend the machine. Save work and keep a recovery TTY
available for initial live testing:

1. Test wrong passwords, retries, rapid Enter presses, and successful authentication.
2. Check short/narrow displays, long feedback, mixed-DPI monitors, and monitor hotplug.
3. Test the shortcut and idle locking, then suspend/resume. Confirm the desktop stays locked
   until authentication succeeds.

shell.qml

quickshell/lockscreen/shell.qml · 350 lines

View on GitHub 
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Services.Pam
import Quickshell.Wayland
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Controls
import QtMultimedia

ShellRoot {
    id: shell

    property string pendingPassword: ""
    property bool firstResponse: false
    property bool waitingForPam: false
    property bool failed: false
    property bool unlocked: false
    property string status: "Enter your password"
    property string videoPath: ""
    readonly property bool busy: unlocked || waitingForPam
    readonly property color accent: unlocked ? "#2ecc71" : failed ? "#f53c3c" : "#7ebae4"

    signal clearInputs()
    signal typed()
    signal rejected()

    Component.onCompleted: Quickshell.watchFiles = false

    IpcHandler {
        target: "lock"
        function isSecure(): bool {
            return lock.secure && !shell.unlocked;
        }
    }

    function submit(password) {
        if (busy || (!password && !pam.active))
            return;
        failed = false;
        waitingForPam = true;
        status = "Checking…";
        if (pam.active && pam.responseRequired) {
            pam.respond(password);
        } else {
            pendingPassword = password;
            firstResponse = true;
            if (!pam.start()) {
                pendingPassword = "";
                firstResponse = false;
                waitingForPam = false;
                failed = true;
                status = "Could not start authentication";
                rejected();
            }
        }
        clearInputs();
    }

    PamContext {
        id: pam
        config: "quickshell-lockscreen"
        onPamMessage: {
            if (responseRequired) {
                if (shell.firstResponse && !responseVisible) {
                    shell.firstResponse = false;
                    const password = shell.pendingPassword;
                    shell.pendingPassword = "";
                    shell.waitingForPam = true;
                    respond(password);
                } else {
                    shell.firstResponse = false;
                    shell.pendingPassword = "";
                    shell.waitingForPam = false;
                    shell.status = message || "Authentication response required";
                }
            } else if (message) {
                shell.status = message;
            }
        }
        onCompleted: function (result) {
            shell.pendingPassword = "";
            shell.firstResponse = false;
            shell.waitingForPam = false;
            shell.clearInputs();
            if (result === PamResult.Success) {
                shell.unlocked = true;
                shell.status = "Unlocked";
                finish.start();
            } else {
                shell.failed = true;
                shell.status = result === PamResult.Error ? "Authentication unavailable" : "Incorrect password";
                shell.rejected();
            }
        }
    }

    SystemClock {
        id: systemClock
        precision: SystemClock.Minutes
    }

    Timer {
        id: finish
        interval: 450
        onTriggered: {
            lock.locked = false;
            Qt.callLater(Qt.quit);
        }
    }

    Process {
        command: ["bash", "-c", "find -H \"$HOME/Wallpapers\" -type f -iname '*.mp4' -readable -print0 2>/dev/null | shuf -z -n 1"]
        running: true
        stdout: StdioCollector {
            onStreamFinished: shell.videoPath = text.endsWith("\u0000") ? text.slice(0, -1) : ""
        }
    }

    // The launcher only returns to swayidle after the compositor confirms the lock.
    Process {
        id: ready
        command: ["touch", Quickshell.env("QUICKSHELL_LOCK_READY")]
        onExited: function (code) {
            if (code !== 0)
                console.error("Could not signal lock readiness; the launcher will report a timeout.");
        }
    }

    WlSessionLock {
        id: lock
        locked: true
        onSecureChanged: {
            if (secure && Quickshell.env("QUICKSHELL_LOCK_READY"))
                ready.running = true;
        }

        WlSessionLockSurface {
            color: "#253b4c"

            Rectangle {
                anchors.fill: parent
                gradient: Gradient {
                    GradientStop { position: 0; color: "#253b4c" }
                    GradientStop { position: 1; color: "#151923" }
                }
            }

            Loader {
                anchors.fill: parent
                active: lock.secure && shell.videoPath !== ""
                sourceComponent: Item {
                    MediaPlayer {
                        id: player
                        source: {
                            const path = shell.videoPath;
                            return path ? "file://" + encodeURIComponent(path).replace(/%2F/gi, "/") : "";
                        }
                        videoOutput: video
                        loops: MediaPlayer.Infinite
                        onErrorOccurred: function (error, errorString) {
                            console.warn("Lockscreen video playback failed:", error, errorString);
                        }
                        onSourceChanged: {
                            if (source && lock.secure)
                                play();
                        }
                    }
                    VideoOutput {
                        id: video
                        anchors.fill: parent
                        fillMode: VideoOutput.PreserveAspectCrop
                        property bool hasFrame: false
                        visible: player.error === MediaPlayer.NoError
                        opacity: hasFrame ? 1 : 0
                        Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: 500; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic } }

                        Connections {
                            target: video.videoSink
                            enabled: !video.hasFrame
                            function onVideoFrameChanged() {
                                if (video.videoSink.videoSize.width > 0 && video.videoSink.videoSize.height > 0)
                                    video.hasFrame = true;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            Rectangle {
                anchors.fill: parent
                color: "#66000000"
            }

            Flickable {
                id: content
                anchors.fill: parent
                contentWidth: width
                contentHeight: Math.max(height, clock.height + clock.gap + card.height + 24)
                interactive: contentHeight > height
                boundsBehavior: Flickable.StopAtBounds
                clip: true
                opacity: shell.unlocked ? 0 : 1
                scale: shell.unlocked ? 1.04 : 1
                Behavior on opacity { NumberAnimation { duration: 400; easing.type: Easing.InCubic } }
                Behavior on scale { NumberAnimation { duration: 400; easing.type: Easing.InCubic } }

                Column {
                    id: clock
                    anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                    width: Math.min(360, Math.max(0, parent.width - 48))
                    readonly property real gap: Math.max(12, Math.min(48, content.height * 0.06))
                    y: Math.max(12, (parent.height - height - card.height - gap) / 2)
                    spacing: 4

                    Text {
                        anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                        text: Qt.formatDateTime(systemClock.date, "HH:mm")
                        color: "#eeeeee"
                        font.pixelSize: Math.max(24, Math.min(88, content.height * 0.14, clock.width * 0.24))
                        font.weight: Font.Light
                    }
                    Text {
                        anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                        text: Qt.formatDateTime(systemClock.date, "dddd, MMMM d")
                        color: "#bfffffff"
                        width: clock.width
                        horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                        wrapMode: Text.WordWrap
                        font.pixelSize: Math.max(12, Math.min(20, content.height * 0.04))
                    }
                }

                Item {
                    id: card
                    width: clock.width
                    height: feedback.y + feedback.height + 24
                    anchors.top: clock.bottom
                    anchors.topMargin: clock.gap
                    anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                    transform: Translate { id: shake }

                    Rectangle {
                        id: indicator
                        anchors.top: parent.top
                        anchors.topMargin: 24
                        anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                        width: Math.min(112, parent.width)
                        height: width
                        radius: width / 2
                        color: "#d90f0f0f"
                        border.width: 4
                        border.color: shell.accent
                        Behavior on border.color { ColorAnimation { duration: 180 } }

                        Rectangle {
                            id: ripple
                            anchors.centerIn: parent
                            width: parent.width
                            height: parent.height
                            radius: parent.radius
                            color: "transparent"
                            border.width: 2
                            border.color: shell.accent
                            opacity: 0
                        }

                        Text {
                            anchors.centerIn: parent
                            text: shell.unlocked ? "✓" : shell.failed ? "!" : shell.waitingForPam ? "…" : ""
                            color: shell.accent
                            font.pixelSize: shell.unlocked ? 24 : 32
                        }
                    }

                    ParallelAnimation {
                        id: pulse
                        NumberAnimation { target: ripple; property: "scale"; from: 1; to: 1.14; duration: 320; easing.type: Easing.OutCubic }
                        NumberAnimation { target: ripple; property: "opacity"; from: 0.7; to: 0; duration: 320 }
                    }

                    SequentialAnimation {
                        id: errorShake
                        NumberAnimation { target: shake; property: "x"; to: -12; duration: 50 }
                        NumberAnimation { target: shake; property: "x"; to: 12; duration: 70 }
                        NumberAnimation { target: shake; property: "x"; to: -8; duration: 70 }
                        NumberAnimation { target: shake; property: "x"; to: 8; duration: 70 }
                        NumberAnimation { target: shake; property: "x"; to: 0; duration: 70 }
                    }

                    Connections {
                        target: shell
                        function onTyped() { pulse.restart(); }
                        function onRejected() { errorShake.restart(); }
                        function onClearInputs() { password.clear(); }
                    }

                    TextField {
                        id: password
                        readonly property bool showResponse: pam.active && pam.responseRequired && pam.responseVisible
                        anchors.top: indicator.bottom
                        anchors.topMargin: showResponse ? 16 : 0
                        anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                        width: Math.max(0, parent.width - 48)
                        height: showResponse ? 48 : 0
                        opacity: showResponse ? 1 : 0
                        focus: true
                        enabled: !shell.busy
                        echoMode: showResponse ? TextInput.Normal : TextInput.Password
                        inputMethodHints: Qt.ImhSensitiveData | Qt.ImhNoPredictiveText | Qt.ImhNoAutoUppercase
                        Component.onCompleted: forceActiveFocus()
                        onEnabledChanged: {
                            if (enabled)
                                forceActiveFocus();
                        }
                        onTextEdited: {
                            if (shell.failed) {
                                shell.failed = false;
                                shell.status = "Enter your password";
                            }
                            shell.typed();
                        }
                        onAccepted: shell.submit(text)
                        Keys.onEscapePressed: {
                            clear();
                            pulse.stop();
                            ripple.opacity = 0;
                            if (shell.failed) {
                                shell.failed = false;
                                shell.status = "Enter your password";
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Text {
                        id: feedback
                        anchors.top: password.bottom
                        anchors.topMargin: 16
                        anchors.horizontalCenter: parent.horizontalCenter
                        width: Math.max(0, parent.width - 24)
                        text: shell.status
                        textFormat: Text.PlainText
                        color: shell.failed ? shell.accent : "#bfffffff"
                        font.pixelSize: 13
                        horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                        wrapMode: Text.WordWrap
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

test-auth.js

quickshell/lockscreen/tests/test-auth.js · 358 lines

// Run the real QML handlers with a PAM mock that keeps responseRequired true
// after respond(), matching Quickshell's implementation.
const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
const fs = require("node:fs");
const path = require("node:path");
const vm = require("node:vm");

const qml = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "..", "shell.qml"), "utf8");
function body(marker) {
  const start = qml.indexOf("{", qml.indexOf(marker));
  assert(qml.includes(marker), `Missing handler: ${marker}`);
  let depth = 1;
  let end = start + 1;
  for (; depth && end < qml.length; end++) {
    if (qml[end] === "{") depth++;
    if (qml[end] === "}") depth--;
  }
  assert.equal(depth, 0);
  return qml.slice(start + 1, end - 1);
}

function createState() {
  const context = {
    pendingPassword: "",
    firstResponse: false,
    waitingForPam: false,
    failed: false,
    unlocked: false,
    status: "Enter your password",
    responses: [],
    clears: 0,
    rejections: 0,
    finishes: 0,
    clearInputs() {
      context.clears++;
    },
    rejected() {
      context.rejections++;
    },
    typed() {},
    PamResult: { Success: 0, Error: 1, Failure: 2 },
  };
  context.shell = context;
  context.finish = {
    start() {
      context.finishes++;
    },
  };
  context.pam = {
    active: false,
    responseRequired: false,
    responseVisible: false,
    start() {
      this.active = true;
      return true;
    },
    respond(value) {
      context.responses.push(value);
    },
  };
  context.respond = (value) => context.pam.respond(value);
  Object.defineProperty(context, "busy", {
    get() {
      return vm.runInContext(
        qml.match(/readonly property bool busy: (.*)/)[1],
        context,
      );
    },
  });
  vm.createContext(context);
  context.submit = (password) => {
    context.password = password;
    vm.runInContext(
      `(() => { ${body("function submit(password)")} })()`,
      context,
    );
  };
  context.sendMessage = (
    responseRequired,
    responseVisible = false,
    message = "Password:",
  ) => {
    Object.assign(context.pam, { responseRequired, responseVisible });
    Object.assign(context, { responseRequired, responseVisible, message });
    vm.runInContext(`(() => { ${body("onPamMessage:")} })()`, context);
  };
  context.complete = (result) => {
    context.pam.active = false;
    context.pam.responseRequired = false;
    context.result = result;
    vm.runInContext(
      `(() => { ${body("onCompleted: function (result)")} })()`,
      context,
    );
  };
  return context;
}

const securityContext = vm.createContext({
  lock: { secure: false },
  shell: { unlocked: false },
});
function querySecureState() {
  return vm.runInContext(
    `(() => { ${body("function isSecure(): bool")} })()`,
    securityContext,
  );
}
assert.equal(querySecureState(), false);
securityContext.lock.secure = true;
assert.equal(querySecureState(), true);
securityContext.shell.unlocked = true;
assert.equal(querySecureState(), false);
securityContext.lock.secure = false;
assert.equal(querySecureState(), false);

  vm.runInContext(`(() => { ${body("onSecureChanged:")} })()`, startupContext);
}

startupContext.secure = true;

assert(qml.includes("if (source && lock.secure)"));
const mediaActiveExpression = qml.match(

assert.equal(state.finishes, 1);
state.submit("after success");

state.sendMessage(true);

state.submit("123456");
assert(state.busy);
state.submit("duplicate OTP");

state.sendMessage(true, false, "Optional response:");
state.submit("");
assert.deepEqual(state.responses, ["password", "123456", ""]);

test_lockscreen.py

quickshell/lockscreen/tests/test_lockscreen.py · 248 lines

"""Standard-library regression tests; no compositor or real locking required."""

import json
import os
import shutil
import signal
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from pathlib import Path


DIRECTORY = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
REPOSITORY = DIRECTORY.parents[1]


class LauncherTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.directory = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="quickshell-lock-test-")
        self.root = Path(self.directory.name)
        self.processes = []
        for name in ("bin", "runtime", "home/Wallpapers"):
            (self.root / name).mkdir(parents=True)
        self.pam = self.root / "pam"
        self.pam.touch()
        source = (REPOSITORY / "sway/scripts/lockscreen.sh").read_text()
        self.script = self.root / "lock.sh"
        self.script.write_text(
            source.replace("/etc/pam.d/quickshell-lockscreen", str(self.pam))
        )
        self.trace = self.root / "trace"
        self.release = self.root / "release"
        self.env = dict(
            os.environ,
            PATH=str(self.root / "bin") + ":" + os.environ["PATH"],
            HOME=str(self.root / "home"),
            XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=str(self.root / "runtime"),
            TEST_TRACE=str(self.trace),
            TEST_RELEASE=str(self.release),
            TEST_REAL_RM=shutil.which("rm"),
            TEST_MODE="ready",
            QUICKSHELL_LOCK_TIMEOUT="3",
        )
        self.env.pop("SWAYLOCK_VIDEO", None)
        self.mock(
            "qs",
            """import json, os, pathlib, sys, time
secure = pathlib.Path(os.environ["TEST_TRACE"] + ".secure")
if sys.argv[1:] == ["-c", "lockscreen", "ipc", "call", "lock", "isSecure"]:
    mode = os.environ.get("TEST_IPC_MODE", "normal")
    if mode == "hang":
        time.sleep(30)
    if mode == "error":
        sys.exit(1)
    print("invalid" if mode == "invalid" else "true" if secure.exists() else "false")
    sys.exit(0)
with open(os.environ["TEST_TRACE"], "a") as f:
    f.write(json.dumps(["qs", os.environ.get("SWAYLOCK_VIDEO", "")]) + "\\n")
mode = os.environ["TEST_MODE"]
if mode == "fail":
    sys.exit(1)
if mode == "marker-failure":
    print("Could not signal lock readiness", flush=True)
while mode in ("hang", "marker-failure", "late-failure") and not pathlib.Path(os.environ["TEST_RELEASE"]).exists():
    time.sleep(0.05)
if mode == "late-failure":
    sys.exit(1)
time.sleep(0.2)
secure.touch()
pathlib.Path(os.environ["QUICKSHELL_LOCK_READY"]).touch()
time.sleep(30)
""",
        )

    def tearDown(self):
        for process in self.processes:
            try:
                os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
            except ProcessLookupError:
                pass
            if process.poll() is None:
                process.wait(timeout=3)
        self.directory.cleanup()

    def mock(self, name, body):
        path = self.root / "bin" / name
        path.write_text("#!/usr/bin/env python3\n" + body)
        path.chmod(0o755)

    def start(self):
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            ["bash", str(self.script)],
            env=self.env,
            start_new_session=True,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            text=True,
        )
        self.processes.append(process)
        return process

    def finish(self, process):
        _, stderr = process.communicate(timeout=6)
        return process.returncode, stderr

    def calls(self):
        return (
            [json.loads(line) for line in self.trace.read_text().splitlines()]
            if self.trace.exists()
            else []
        )

    def test_readiness_duplicate_requests_without_wallpaper_scanning(self):
        (self.root / "home/Wallpapers/space # & ' ü.mp4").touch()
        self.mock(
            "find",
            """raise SystemExit("Video scan must not precede locking")
""",
        )
        started = time.monotonic()
        first = self.start()
        second = self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(first)[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(second)[0], 0)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(time.monotonic() - started, 0.2)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.calls()), 1)

    def test_standard_swayidle_sleep_integration(self):
        sway = (REPOSITORY / "sway/config").read_text()
        self.assertIn("exec swayidle -w", sway)
        self.assertRegex(sway, r"before-sleep.*lockscreen\.sh")
        configuration = (REPOSITORY / "nixos/configuration.nix").read_text()
        self.assertNotIn("systemd.user.services.lockscreen", configuration)
        self.assertNotIn("systemd.services.lock-before-sleep", configuration)
        layout = (REPOSITORY / "wlogout/layout").read_text()
        self.assertIn('"action" : "systemctl suspend"', layout)
        self.assertIn('"action" : "systemctl hibernate"', layout)

    def test_no_video(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])

    def test_startup_failure_is_not_success(self):
        self.env["TEST_MODE"] = "fail"
        code, error = self.finish(self.start())
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("Quickshell lock failed", error)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])

    def test_missing_quickshell_is_not_success(self):
        (self.root / "bin/qs").unlink()
        for command in ("bash", "flock"):
            (self.root / "bin" / command).symlink_to(shutil.which(command))
        self.env["PATH"] = str(self.root / "bin")
        code, error = self.finish(self.start())
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("Quickshell and its PAM service are required", error)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [])

    def test_missing_pam_is_not_success(self):
        self.pam.unlink()
        code, error = self.finish(self.start())
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("Quickshell and its PAM service are required", error)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [])

    def test_timeout_keeps_startup_guard_and_allows_late_readiness(self):
        self.env.update(TEST_MODE="hang", QUICKSHELL_LOCK_TIMEOUT="1")
        code, error = self.finish(self.start())
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("readiness timed out", error)
        code, error = self.finish(self.start())
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("another lock startup", error)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])
        self.release.touch()
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])

    def test_marker_failure_is_bounded(self):
        self.env.update(TEST_MODE="marker-failure", QUICKSHELL_LOCK_TIMEOUT="1")
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])
        log = (self.root / "runtime/quickshell-lockscreen.log").read_text()
        self.assertIn("Could not signal lock readiness", log)

    def test_late_failure_keeps_startup_exclusion_through_cleanup(self):
        self.env.update(TEST_MODE="late-failure", QUICKSHELL_LOCK_TIMEOUT="1")
        self.mock(
            "rm",
            """import os, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time
pathlib.Path(os.environ["TEST_TRACE"] + ".cleanup").touch()
while not pathlib.Path(os.environ["TEST_RELEASE"] + ".cleanup").exists():
    time.sleep(0.02)
subprocess.run([os.environ["TEST_REAL_RM"], *sys.argv[1:]], check=True)
""",
        )
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 1)
        self.release.touch()
        cleanup_started = Path(str(self.trace) + ".cleanup")
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 3
        while not cleanup_started.exists():
            self.assertLess(time.monotonic(), deadline, "Cleanup did not start")
            time.sleep(0.02)
        code, error = self.finish(self.start())
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("another lock startup", error)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])
        Path(str(self.release) + ".cleanup").touch()
        self.env.update(TEST_MODE="ready", QUICKSHELL_LOCK_TIMEOUT="3")
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""], ["qs", ""]])

    def test_lifecycle_guard_after_unlock_is_not_readiness(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 0)
        Path(str(self.trace) + ".secure").unlink()
        code, error = self.finish(self.start())
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)
        self.assertIn("secure state could not be confirmed", error)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])

    def test_duplicate_ipc_failures_do_not_report_success(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 0)
        self.env["QUICKSHELL_LOCK_TIMEOUT"] = "1"
        for mode in ("error", "invalid", "hang"):
            with self.subTest(mode=mode):
                self.env["TEST_IPC_MODE"] = mode
                code, error = self.finish(self.start())
                self.assertEqual(code, 1)
                self.assertIn("secure state could not be confirmed", error)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [["qs", ""]])

    def test_invalid_timeout(self):
        self.env["QUICKSHELL_LOCK_TIMEOUT"] = "0"
        self.assertEqual(self.finish(self.start())[0], 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls(), [])

    unittest.main()

apply.sh

quickshell/wallpaper-picker/apply.sh · 46 lines

View on GitHub 
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

frame0=false
if [[ ${1:-} == --frame0 ]]; then
  frame0=true
  shift
fi
selected=${1:?Usage: apply.sh [--frame0] WALLPAPER}
[[ -f "$selected" ]] || exit 1

STATE_DIR="${XDG_STATE_HOME:-$HOME/.local/state}/wallpaper"
VIDEO_WALLPAPER_LINK="$STATE_DIR/current-video"
if "$frame0"; then
  script_dir=$(
    CDPATH=
    cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd
  )
  selected=$(bash "$script_dir/extract-frame.sh" "$selected")
fi
mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"
printf '%s\n' "$selected" >"$STATE_DIR/last-wallpaper"
mime_type=$(file --brief --mime-type -- "$selected")

case "$mime_type" in
image/*)
  systemctl --user stop wallpaper-video.service
  swaymsg output '*' bg "$selected" fill
  ;;
video/*)
  mapfile -t swaybg_pids < <(pgrep -f '[s]waybg' || true)
  if ((${#swaybg_pids[@]})); then
    kill "${swaybg_pids[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true
    for _ in {1..10}; do
      swaybg_running=false
      for pid in "${swaybg_pids[@]}"; do
        kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null && swaybg_running=true
      done
      "$swaybg_running" || break
      sleep 0.05
    done
  fi
  ln -sfn -- "$selected" "$VIDEO_WALLPAPER_LINK"
  systemctl --user restart wallpaper-video.service
  ;;
esac

extract-frame.sh

quickshell/wallpaper-picker/extract-frame.sh · 21 lines

View on GitHub 
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

video=${1:?Usage: extract-frame.sh VIDEO}
[[ -f "$video" ]] || exit 1

STATE_DIR="${XDG_STATE_HOME:-$HOME/.local/state}/wallpaper"
FRAME_DIR="$STATE_DIR/video-frames"
mkdir -p "$FRAME_DIR"
key=$(printf '%s\0%s' "$video" "$(stat -c '%y:%s' -- "$video")" | sha256sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
frame="$FRAME_DIR/$key.png"
if [[ ! -f "$frame" ]]; then
  temporary=$(mktemp "$FRAME_DIR/.${key}.tmp.XXXXXX.png")
  trap 'rm -f -- "$temporary"' EXIT
  ffmpeg -y -hide_banner -loglevel error \
    -i "$video" -map 0:v:0 -frames:v 1 "$temporary"
  mv -- "$temporary" "$frame"
  trap - EXIT
fi

printf '%s\n' "$frame"

scan.py

quickshell/wallpaper-picker/scan.py · 169 lines

View on GitHub 
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Stream wallpaper entries and cached thumbnails to Quickshell."""

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from pathlib import Path

IMAGES = {".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp", ".gif", ".bmp", ".tif", ".tiff", ".avif"}
VIDEOS = {".mp4", ".mkv", ".webm", ".mov", ".avi"}


def emit(entry):
    print(json.dumps(entry), flush=True)


def small_thumbnail_path(path, cache):
    key = hashlib.sha256(
        (str(path) + str(path.stat().st_mtime_ns) + ":small-v1").encode()
    )
    return cache / (key.hexdigest() + ".png")


def thumbnail(path, target, video, source):
    if not target.is_file():
        temporary = target.with_suffix(".tmp.png")
        command = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error"]
        if video and source is None:
            command += ["-ss", "1"]
        command += [
            "-i",
            str(source or path),
            "-vf",
            "scale=200:120:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease",
            "-frames:v",
            "1",
            str(temporary),
        ]
        result = subprocess.run(
            command,
            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            check=False,
        )
        if result.returncode != 0:
            temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            return None
        temporary.replace(target)
    return str(target)


def video_preview(path, target):
    temporary = target.with_suffix(".tmp.png")
    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            "ffmpeg",
            "-y",
            "-hide_banner",
            "-loglevel",
            "error",
            "-ss",
            "1",
            "-i",
            str(path),
            "-frames:v",
            "1",
            str(temporary),
        ],
        stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        check=False,
    )
    if result.returncode != 0:
        temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        return None
    temporary.replace(target)
    return str(target)


def main(directory):
    cache = (
        Path(os.environ.get("XDG_CACHE_HOME", Path.home() / ".cache"))
        / "wallpaper-picker"
    )
    pending = []
    previews = []
    try:
        entries = sorted(
            Path(directory).iterdir(), key=lambda path: path.name.casefold()
        )
    except OSError as error:
        emit({"error": str(error)})
        return

    for path in entries:
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        path = path.resolve()
        suffix = path.suffix.lower()
        if suffix in IMAGES:
            cached = small_thumbnail_path(path, cache)
            ready = cached.is_file()
            emit(
                {
                    "path": str(path),
                    "thumbnail": str(cached) if ready else str(path),
                    "video": False,
                    "small": ready,
                    "preview": str(path),
                }
            )
            if not ready:
                pending.append((path, cached, False, None))
        elif suffix in VIDEOS:
            cached = small_thumbnail_path(path, cache)
            ready = cached.is_file()
            key = hashlib.sha256(
                (str(path) + ":full-resolution-v1").encode()
            ).hexdigest()
            old = cache / (key + ".png")
            old_ready = (
                old.is_file() and old.stat().st_mtime_ns >= path.stat().st_mtime_ns
            )
            emit(
                {
                    "path": str(path),
                    "thumbnail": str(cached)
                    if ready
                    else str(old)
                    if old_ready
                    else "",
                    "preview": str(old) if old_ready else str(cached) if ready else "",
                    "video": True,
                    "small": ready,
                }
            )
            if not ready:
                pending.append((path, cached, True, old if old_ready else None))
            if not old_ready:
                previews.append((path, old))

    if pending or previews:
        cache.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            futures = {
                pool.submit(thumbnail, path, target, video, source): (path, "thumbnail")
                for path, target, video, source in pending
            }
            futures.update(
                {
                    pool.submit(video_preview, path, target): (path, "preview")
                    for path, target in previews
                }
            )
            for future in as_completed(futures):
                result = future.result()
                if result:
                    path, role = futures[future]
                    update = {"path": str(path), role: result}
                    if role == "thumbnail":
                        update["small"] = True
                    emit(update)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main(sys.argv[1])

shell.qml

quickshell/wallpaper-picker/shell.qml · 390 lines

View on GitHub 
import Quickshell
import Quickshell.Io
import Quickshell.Wayland
import QtQuick
import QtQuick.Controls
import QtQuick.Layouts
import QtMultimedia

ShellRoot {
    id: shell

    property string directory: Quickshell.env("HOME") + "/Wallpapers"
    property string selectedPath: ""
    property bool selectedVideo: false
    property string selectedThumbnail: ""
    property string selectedPreview: ""
    property string scanError: ""
    property bool closeAfterApply: false

    function fileUrl(path) {
        return path ? "file://" + encodeURIComponent(path).replace(/%2F/gi, "/") : "";
    }

    function open() {
        if (panel.visible) {
            list.forceActiveFocus();
            return;
        }
        wallpapers.clear();
        shell.selectedPath = "";
        shell.selectedThumbnail = "";
        shell.selectedPreview = "";
        shell.scanError = "";
        scanner.running = true;
        panel.visible = true;
        list.forceActiveFocus();
    }

    function close() {
        panel.visible = false;
        videoDelay.stop();
        player.stop();
        player.source = "";
        scanner.running = false;
        framePrepareDelay.stop();
        prepareFrame.running = false;
    }

    function applyWallpaper(path, frame0) {
        if (!path || apply.running)
            return;
        if (frame0) {
            framePrepareDelay.stop();
            prepareFrame.running = false;
        }
        apply.command = ["bash", Qt.resolvedUrl("apply.sh").toString().replace("file://", "")];
        if (frame0)
            apply.command.push("--frame0");
        apply.command.push(path);
        shell.closeAfterApply = frame0;
        apply.running = true;
        if (!frame0)
            shell.close();
    }

    function select(index) {
        if (wallpapers.count === 0 || index < 0 || index >= wallpapers.count)
            return;
        list.currentIndex = index;
        const entry = wallpapers.get(index);
        shell.selectedPath = entry.path;
        shell.selectedVideo = entry.video;
        shell.selectedThumbnail = entry.thumbnail;
        shell.selectedPreview = entry.preview || entry.thumbnail;
        framePrepareDelay.stop();
        prepareFrame.running = false;
        if (entry.video)
            framePrepareDelay.start();
        videoDelay.stop();
        if (player.source) {
            player.stop();
            player.source = "";
        }
        if (entry.video)
            videoDelay.start();
    }

    IpcHandler {
        target: "picker"
        function toggle(): void {
            if (panel.visible)
                shell.close();
            else
                shell.open();
        }
    }

    ListModel {
        id: wallpapers
    }

    Process {
        id: scanner
        command: ["python3", Qt.resolvedUrl("scan.py").toString().replace("file://", ""), shell.directory]
        stdout: SplitParser {
            onRead: function (line) {
                if (!panel.visible)
                    return;
                try {
                    const entry = JSON.parse(line);
                    if (entry.error) {
                        shell.scanError = entry.error;
                    } else if (entry.video !== undefined) {
                        wallpapers.append(entry);
                        if (wallpapers.count === 1)
                            shell.select(0);
                    } else {
                        for (let i = 0; i < wallpapers.count; i++) {
                            if (wallpapers.get(i).path === entry.path) {
                                if (entry.thumbnail !== undefined) {
                                    wallpapers.setProperty(i, "thumbnail", entry.thumbnail);
                                    wallpapers.setProperty(i, "small", entry.small);
                                    if (shell.selectedPath === entry.path)
                                        shell.selectedThumbnail = entry.thumbnail;
                                    if (!wallpapers.get(i).preview) {
                                        wallpapers.setProperty(i, "preview", entry.thumbnail);
                                        if (shell.selectedPath === entry.path)
                                            shell.selectedPreview = entry.thumbnail;
                                    }
                                }
                                if (entry.preview !== undefined) {
                                    wallpapers.setProperty(i, "preview", entry.preview);
                                    if (shell.selectedPath === entry.path)
                                        shell.selectedPreview = entry.preview;
                                }
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (error) {
                    console.warn("Wallpaper scan:", error);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Process {
        id: prepareFrame
        onExited: function (code) {
            if (code !== 0)
                console.warn("Could not prepare video frame (exit " + code + ")");
        }
    }

    Process {
        id: apply
        onExited: function (code) {
            if (code === 0 && shell.closeAfterApply) {
                shell.closeAfterApply = false;
                shell.close();
            } else if (code !== 0) {
                shell.closeAfterApply = false;
                console.warn("Could not apply wallpaper (exit " + code + ")");
            }
        }
    }

    Timer {
        id: framePrepareDelay
        interval: 400
        onTriggered: {
            if (panel.visible && shell.selectedVideo) {
                prepareFrame.command = ["bash", Qt.resolvedUrl("extract-frame.sh").toString().replace("file://", ""), shell.selectedPath];
                prepareFrame.running = true;
            }
        }
    }

    Timer {
        id: videoDelay
        interval: 600
        onTriggered: {
            if (panel.visible && shell.selectedVideo) {
                player.source = shell.fileUrl(shell.selectedPath);
                player.play();
            }
        }
    }

    MediaPlayer {
        id: player
        videoOutput: videoOutput
        loops: MediaPlayer.Infinite
    }

    PanelWindow {
        id: panel
        anchors {
            top: true
            bottom: true
            left: true
            right: true
        }
        exclusiveZone: 0
        WlrLayershell.keyboardFocus: visible ? WlrKeyboardFocus.Exclusive : WlrKeyboardFocus.None
        color: "transparent"
        visible: false
        onVisibleChanged: {
            if (visible)
                Qt.callLater(function () {
                    list.forceActiveFocus();
                });
        }

        Shortcut {
            sequence: "Ctrl+N"
            enabled: panel.visible
            onActivated: shell.select((list.currentIndex + 1) % wallpapers.count)
        }
        Shortcut {
            sequence: "Ctrl+P"
            enabled: panel.visible
            onActivated: shell.select((list.currentIndex - 1 + wallpapers.count) % wallpapers.count)
        }

        FocusScope {
            id: keyboard
            anchors.fill: parent
            focus: true
            Keys.onPressed: function (event) {
                if (event.key === Qt.Key_Escape)
                    shell.close();
                else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Return || event.key === Qt.Key_Enter) {
                    if (shell.selectedPath)
                        shell.applyWallpaper(shell.selectedPath, false);
                } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_F && shell.selectedVideo) {
                    shell.applyWallpaper(shell.selectedPath, true);
                } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Down || event.key === Qt.Key_Right || (event.modifiers & Qt.ControlModifier && event.key === Qt.Key_N)) {
                    shell.select((list.currentIndex + 1) % wallpapers.count);
                } else if (event.key === Qt.Key_Up || event.key === Qt.Key_Left || (event.modifiers & Qt.ControlModifier && event.key === Qt.Key_P)) {
                    shell.select((list.currentIndex - 1 + wallpapers.count) % wallpapers.count);
                } else
                    return;
                event.accepted = true;
            }

            Rectangle {
                anchors.fill: parent
                color: "#99000000"
                MouseArea {
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    onClicked: shell.close()
                }
            }

            Rectangle {
                width: Math.min(1200, parent.width - 48)
                height: Math.min(760, parent.height - 48)
                anchors.centerIn: parent
                radius: 12
                color: "#f20f0f0f"
                border.color: "#1fffffff"

                ColumnLayout {
                    anchors.fill: parent
                    anchors.margins: 16
                    spacing: 12

                    RowLayout {
                        Layout.fillWidth: true
                        Layout.fillHeight: true
                        spacing: 16

                        ListView {
                            id: list
                            focus: true
                            onCurrentIndexChanged: {
                                if (currentIndex >= 0 && currentIndex < wallpapers.count && shell.selectedPath !== wallpapers.get(currentIndex).path)
                                    shell.select(currentIndex);
                            }
                            Layout.preferredWidth: 120
                            Layout.fillHeight: true
                            clip: true
                            spacing: 12
                            model: wallpapers
                            highlightMoveDuration: 0
                            delegate: Rectangle {
                                required property string path
                                required property string thumbnail
                                required property bool small
                                required property int index
                                width: list.width
                                height: 90
                                radius: 6
                                color: index === list.currentIndex ? "#64727d" : "#26ffffff"

                                Image {
                                    anchors.fill: parent
                                    anchors.margins: 4
                                    source: shell.fileUrl(thumbnail)
                                    sourceSize.width: 200
                                    sourceSize.height: 120
                                    fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectFit
                                    asynchronous: !small
                                }
                                MouseArea {
                                    anchors.fill: parent
                                    onClicked: {
                                        shell.select(index);
                                        list.forceActiveFocus();
                                    }
                                    onDoubleClicked: {
                                        shell.select(index);
                                        shell.applyWallpaper(path, false);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        Item {
                            Layout.fillWidth: true
                            Layout.fillHeight: true
                            Image {
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                source: shell.fileUrl(shell.selectedPreview)
                                sourceSize.width: 1200
                                sourceSize.height: 760
                                cache: false
                                fillMode: Image.PreserveAspectFit
                                asynchronous: true
                                visible: player.playbackState !== MediaPlayer.PlayingState
                            }
                            VideoOutput {
                                id: videoOutput
                                anchors.fill: parent
                                fillMode: VideoOutput.PreserveAspectFit
                                visible: player.playbackState === MediaPlayer.PlayingState
                            }
                            Label {
                                id: message
                                anchors.centerIn: parent
                                visible: !shell.selectedPath
                                text: shell.scanError || "No wallpapers found"
                                color: "#eeeeee"
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    RowLayout {
                        Layout.alignment: Qt.AlignHCenter
                        spacing: 12

                        Button {
                            id: frameButton
                            visible: shell.selectedVideo
                            text: "Use image from video"
                            padding: 8
                            enabled: !apply.running
                            onClicked: shell.applyWallpaper(shell.selectedPath, true)

                            background: Rectangle {
                                implicitWidth: 110
                                implicitHeight: 32
                                radius: 6
                                color: frameButton.down
                                    ? "#64727d"
                                    : frameButton.hovered ? "#3dffffff" : "#26ffffff"
                                border.color: "#1fffffff"
                            }

                            contentItem: Text {
                                text: frameButton.text
                                color: "#eeeeee"
                                horizontalAlignment: Text.AlignHCenter
                                verticalAlignment: Text.AlignVCenter
                            }
                        }

                        Label {
                            text: shell.selectedVideo
                                ? "F: use image from video · Enter: use video · Escape to cancel"
                                : "Arrow keys to navigate · Enter to select · Escape to cancel"
                            color: "#aaaaaa"
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

configuration {
  modi: "drun,run,window";
  show-icons: true;
  display-drun: "Applications";
  drun-match-fields: "name";
  display-run: "Run";
  display-window: "Windows";
}

* {
  background: rgba(15, 15, 15, 94%);
  surface: rgba(255, 255, 255, 6%);
  surface-hover: rgba(126, 186, 228, 12%);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 12%);
  foreground: #eeeeee;
  muted: rgba(255, 255, 255, 55%);
  accent: #7ebae4;
  critical: #f53c3c;
  background-color: transparent;
  text-color: @foreground;
  font: "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font 11";
}

window {
  width: 600px;

build_mac.sh

scripts/build_mac.sh · 63 lines

View on GitHub 
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

CWD=$(pwd)

if [ -z "$1" ]; then
  echo "Please provide the directory where emacs source is located"
  exit 1
fi

EMACS_REPO_DIR=$1

if [ ! -d "$EMACS_REPO_DIR" ]; then
  echo "$EMACS_REPO_DIR is not a valid directory"
  exit 1
fi

cd "$EMACS_REPO_DIR" || exit

# clean past build data
git clean -fdx

make clean

echo "Making sure all requirements are installed..."

# harfbuzz adds support for ligatures, this is required by ligature.el package
brew install autoconf automake libxml2 gnutls cmake librsvg texinfo libgccjit

echo "Seting up variables..."

make configure
./configure --with-ns --with-xwidgets --with-tree-sitter
make -j install

echo "Compilation done!"

echo "Move compiled application into Applications folder?"
read -r answer

# we ask for confirmation so we can check compilation stdout before replacing a working version of emacs
if [ "$answer" != "${answer#[Yy]}" ]; then
  echo "Compiled application was moved into Applications folder"
  # Copy app in Applications folder
  cp -rf nextstep/Emacs.app /Applications/

  # Set custom icon that fits Bug Sur design
  # copied from https://github.com/mklement0/fileicon/blob/master/bin/fileicon
  osascript <<EOF
    use framework "Cocoa"

    set sourcePath to "$CWD/Emacs.icns"
    set destPath to "/Applications/Emacs.app"

    set imageData to (current application's NSImage's alloc()'s initWithContentsOfFile:sourcePath)
    (current application's NSWorkspace's sharedWorkspace()'s setIcon:imageData forFile:destPath options:2)
EOF
else
  echo "Compiled application wasn't moved"
  exit 1
fi

cd - || exit

generate-config-site.py

scripts/generate-config-site.py · 377 lines

View on GitHub 
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate a single-page site from the repository's config files."""

import argparse
import fnmatch
import hashlib
import html
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import quote

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
EMACS_SOURCE = Path(".emacs.d/bootstrap.org")
EMACS_RENDERER = ROOT / "scripts" / "render-emacs-config-to-html.sh"
STYLESHEET = ROOT / "scripts" / "config-site.css"
MAX_FILE_BYTES = 1_000_000
DEFAULT_EXCLUDES = (
    ".DS_Store",
    "*.lock",
    "*.db",
    "*.elc",
    "*.png",
    "*.jpg",
    "*.jpeg",
    "*.gif",
    "*.webp",
    "*.icns",
    "*.woff*",
    "*.ttf",
    "*.pdf",
    ".env*",
    "*credentials*",
    "*secret*",
    "id_rsa*",
    "site/**",
    "scripts/output/**",
    "**/__pycache__/**",
)

LANGUAGES = {
    ".css": "css",
    ".el": "lisp",
    ".html": "markup",
    ".ini": "ini",
    ".json": "json",
    ".jsonc": "json",
    ".lua": "lua",
    ".md": "markdown",
    ".nix": "nix",
    ".org": "markup",
    ".rasi": "css",
    ".scss": "scss",
    ".sh": "bash",
    ".toml": "toml",
    ".xml": "markup",
    ".yaml": "yaml",
    ".yml": "yaml",
    ".yuck": "lisp",
}

SPECIAL_LANGUAGES = {
    ".gitconfig": "ini",
    ".ideavimrc": "vim",
    ".tmux.conf": "bash",
    ".vimrc": "vim",
    ".Xmodmap": "text",
}

GROUP_NAMES = {
    ".emacs.d": "Emacs",
    ".github": "GitHub",
    ".pi": "Pi",
    "nix-darwin": "Nix Darwin",
    "nixos": "NixOS",
    "zsh": "Zsh",
}


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument(
        "-o",
        "--output",
        type=Path,
        default=ROOT / "site" / "index.html",
        help="output HTML path (default: site/index.html)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--exclude",
        action="append",
        default=[],
        metavar="GLOB",
        help="additional repository-relative glob to exclude; repeatable",
    )
    return parser.parse_args()


def git_output(*arguments: str) -> str:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["git", *arguments],
            cwd=ROOT,
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        )
    except FileNotFoundError as error:
        raise RuntimeError("Git is required to generate the site") from error
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as error:
        detail = error.stderr.strip()
        raise RuntimeError(detail or f"git {' '.join(arguments)} failed") from error
    return result.stdout.strip()


def github_source_base() -> str:
    repository = os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY")
    if repository is None:
        remote = git_output("remote", "get-url", "origin")
        marker = "github.com"
        if marker not in remote:
            raise RuntimeError("the origin remote is not hosted on GitHub")
        repository = remote.split(marker, 1)[1].lstrip(":/").removesuffix(".git")

    revision = os.environ.get("GITHUB_SHA") or git_output("rev-parse", "HEAD")
    return f"https://github.com/{repository}/blob/{revision}/"


def repository_files() -> list[Path]:
    """Return tracked files, failing closed outside a Git checkout."""
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "ls-files", "-z"],
            cwd=ROOT,
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
        )
    except FileNotFoundError as error:
        raise RuntimeError("Git is required to discover publishable files") from error
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as error:
        detail = error.stderr.decode(errors="replace").strip()
        message = "not inside a Git repository"
        raise RuntimeError(f"could not list tracked files: {detail or message}") from error

    return [ROOT / os.fsdecode(item) for item in result.stdout.split(b"\0") if item]


def is_excluded(relative: Path, patterns: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
    name = relative.as_posix()
    return any(
        fnmatch.fnmatch(name, pattern) or fnmatch.fnmatch(relative.name, pattern)
        for pattern in patterns
    )


def read_text(path: Path) -> str | None:
    try:
        data = path.read_bytes()
    except OSError as error:
        print(f"warning: skipping {path}: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return None
    if len(data) > MAX_FILE_BYTES:
        print(f"warning: skipping {path}: larger than {MAX_FILE_BYTES:,} bytes", file=sys.stderr)
        return None
    if b"\0" in data:
        return None
    try:
        return data.decode("utf-8")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        return None


def group_name(relative: Path) -> str:
    if len(relative.parts) == 1:
        return "General"
    first = relative.parts[0]
    return GROUP_NAMES.get(first, first.lstrip(".").replace("-", " ").title())


def language_for(path: Path, source: str) -> str:
    if path.name in SPECIAL_LANGUAGES:
        return SPECIAL_LANGUAGES[path.name]
    if path.suffix in LANGUAGES:
        return LANGUAGES[path.suffix]
    if source.startswith("#!") and "sh" in source.splitlines()[0]:
        return "bash"
    return "text"


def file_id(relative: Path) -> str:
    path = relative.as_posix()
    slug = "".join(char if char.isalnum() else "-" for char in path).strip("-")
    digest = hashlib.sha256(path.encode()).hexdigest()[:10]
    return f"file-{slug}-{digest}"


def render_file(relative: Path, source: str, source_base: str) -> str:
    path = relative.as_posix()
    source_url = source_base + quote(path, safe="/")
    line_count = len(source.splitlines())
    language = language_for(relative, source)
    return f"""
<article class="file" id="{file_id(relative)}">
  <header class="file-header">
    <div>
      <h3>{html.escape(relative.name)}</h3>
      <p>{html.escape(path)} · {line_count:,} lines</p>
    </div>
    <div class="file-actions">
      <a class="source-link" href="{html.escape(source_url, quote=True)}">View on GitHub</a>
      <button class="copy" type="button" aria-label="Copy {html.escape(path, quote=True)}">Copy</button>
    </div>
  </header>
  <pre><code class="language-{language}">{html.escape(source)}</code></pre>
</article>"""


def render_site(
    files: list[tuple[Path, str]], stylesheet: str, source_base: str
) -> str:
    grouped: dict[str, list[tuple[Path, str]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for relative, source in files:
        grouped[group_name(relative)].append((relative, source))

    navigation = []
    sections = []
    for group in sorted(grouped, key=str.casefold):
        entries = sorted(grouped[group], key=lambda item: item[0].as_posix().casefold())
        group_id = "group-" + group.lower().replace(" ", "-")
        if group == "Emacs":
            navigation.append(
                '<a href="emacs.html"><span>Emacs guide</span><b>↗</b></a>'
            )
            heading = '<a href="emacs.html">Emacs <small>Open documented config →</small></a>'
        else:
            navigation.append(
                f'<a href="#{group_id}"><span>{html.escape(group)}</span><b>{len(entries)}</b></a>'
            )
            heading = html.escape(group)
        cards = "\n".join(
            render_file(path, source, source_base) for path, source in entries
        )
        sections.append(
            f'<section class="group" id="{group_id}"><h2>{heading}</h2>{cards}</section>'
        )

    return TEMPLATE.replace("{{NAVIGATION}}", "\n".join(navigation)).replace(
        "{{SECTIONS}}", "\n".join(sections)
    ).replace("147", str(len(files))).replace(
        "28", str(len(grouped))
    ).replace(":root { color-scheme: dark; --bg: #111318; --panel: #191c23; --sidebar: #15171d; --muted: #969dab; --text: #e6e9ef; --accent: #8aadf4; --border: #2c313c; }
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
html { scroll-behavior: smooth; }
body { margin: 0; background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); font: 15px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; }
a { color: inherit; text-decoration: none; }

/* Dotfiles index */
aside { position: fixed; inset: 0 auto 0 0; width: 270px; padding: 28px 20px; overflow: auto; border-right: 1px solid var(--border); background: var(--sidebar); }
aside h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 1.4rem; }
aside > p { margin: 4px 0 20px; color: var(--muted); font-size: .85rem; }
nav a { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; padding: 7px 9px; border-radius: 6px; color: var(--muted); }
nav a:hover { color: var(--text); background: var(--panel); }
nav b { color: var(--accent); font-size: .75rem; }
.nav-toggle { display: none; width: 100%; margin-bottom: 10px; padding: 8px 10px; color: var(--text); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 6px; background: var(--panel); text-align: left; cursor: pointer; }
